B. Sperm capacitation - This is the process of sperm becoming capable of fertilization, involving changes in membrane fluidity, calcium signaling, etc.
C. Sperm binding to the zona pellucida - This involves proteins like ZP3 receptors on sperm surface
D. Fusion of sperm to the egg plasma membrane involved in single fertilization - This is the actual membrane fusion event, which IZUMO1/FOLR4 is directly involved in

IZUMO1 is THE protein that mediates the fusion of the sperm plasma membrane with the egg plasma membrane. It interacts with JUNO (IZUMO1R) on the egg surface. This is specifically the &quot;Fusion of sperm to the egg plasma membrane involved in single fertilization&quot; process.

The question asks which label &quot;most specifically describes its process association, rather than a neighboring event or an indirect prerequisite.&quot; 

Acrosome reaction and capacitation are prerequisites/earlier events. Sperm binding to zona pellucida is also an earlier step. The fusion event is what IZUMO1 directly participates in.

So the answer should be D.
</pre>

</details>
